# Phase 2 — Relocation Ledger

Authoritative per-file record of the Phase-2 documentation migration. Generated
from the Stage-1 classification plus the actual `git mv` results on branch
`claude/symbolu-docs-migration-phase2`.

## Outcome totals

- **MOVED**: 1646
- **EXCLUDED_HYBRID**: 982
- **EXCLUDED_PACKAGE**: 259
- **DEFERRED_REVIEW**: 251
- **ACTIVE_PRODUCT_KEEP**: 118
- **STUBBED_AND_MOVED**: 94
- **ACTIVE_APP_KEEP**: 72
- **KEPT**: 10
- **JUSTIFIED_DEVIATION**: 5

Terminal-state key: `MOVED`/`STUBBED_AND_MOVED` = relocated under `Project_documentation/`; `JUSTIFIED_DEVIATION` = kept at canonical path because a CI/operational consumer references it by exact path; `DEFERRED_REVIEW` = Stage-1 REVIEW/REVIEW_REQUIRED left in place; `KEPT` = infra/tooling/root docs kept in place; `ACTIVE_PACKAGE`/`ACTIVE_PRODUCT_KEEP`/`ACTIVE_APP_KEEP`/`EXCLUDED_*` = non-legacy boundaries never migrated.

## Scope correction (Phase-2 update)

`apps/**` and `products/**` are ACTIVE implementation boundaries, not legacy. Stage-1 `MOVE`/`STUB_AND_MOVE` entries under `products/**` (118 files) were overridden to `ACTIVE_PRODUCT_KEEP`; `apps/**` docs remain colocated (`ACTIVE_APP_KEEP`). None were moved.
